Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a proofs algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 704. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 121/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 704 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the proofs structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 121. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
